## Description

Factorio Post Apocalypse #24 PRODUCTION SCIENCE

194 Likes

3,913 Views

2019 Sep 29

WELCOME TO THE POST-APOCALYPSE FACTORIO! This Lets Play series is an unmodded (Vanilla) series based on the scenario "Post Collapse Nauvis" by lilstrip/Maelstrom[#6959](http://www.youtube.com/hashtag/6959). We are making planetfall on Nauvis after an unknown threat has annihilated the population. Our objective is to cleanse the capital and re-establish a base of operation. Post Collapse Nauvis Scenario: This is a gigantic map created by lilstrip/Maelstrom[#6959](http://www.youtube.com/hashtag/6959). The scenario was highlighted in a Friday Fact blog and is available for download here: [http://www.mediafire.com/file/jpj5qlb...](https://www.youtube.com/redirect?event=video_description&redir_token=QUM4Zm9rUzJTblRWd3BXNWwzS1JaM2FUUzI5cHxBR3JiS2FtcTViM0paa28tc01xWlllckhlQzRseTU4Q0lvSEVkckl5M3lUMmNpVkEyQmJaR21nQi1iejNQR3N2OEEwZkdnVHAwcGx0bU51bFBROGx5aTNNQnA2M0J2ODVnYVpr&q=http%3A%2F%2Fwww.mediafire.com%2Ffile%2Fjpj5qlbqgzpudn6%2FNauvis_Post_Collapse.zip%2Ffile&v=rDvowtkQuBY) The map is VERY large and requires good hardware to run smoothly. More information here: [https://forums.factorio.com/viewtopic...](https://www.youtube.com/redirect?event=video_description&redir_token=QUM4Zm9rUU90aE5xTG90eG1BNGNHNldMTkdSbXxBR3JiS2FuQUt0RW4wWVZTV0NCeDZnYXJnaU9kdkpqdnBiVjZ6ZDNiSkZWbDg1N2NJTExYSzhGNV9mbkpBREhtRDZrOTZzRUlCeFM1bWhYYnpOdzVqVG45eHE4bjhxZVY4MmYx&q=https%3A%2F%2Fforums.factorio.com%2Fviewtopic.php%3Ft%3D74597&v=rDvowtkQuBY) Schedule: New episodes 4 times / week: Monday, Wednesday, Friday and Sunday at [18:00](http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=rDvowtkQuBY&t=1080s) CEST Blueprints: [https://nilaus.atlassian.net/wiki/spa...](https://www.youtube.com/redirect?event=video_description&redir_token=QUM4Zm9rVGhPdXhXQUYxUlNpN0d1Q3BRNGNUenxBR3JiS2FscFRETjk3QlF4dDJacDY3X1FOQ2dpdC13OGVyaGZoLWFJTE9fNWU4MjNzU2h5dzRHakEtS0QzdXVSNUtYR2pCUmNpYnd1bXZaVDV5MllTdHVQcVBPeFVaUzdFX1Ju&q=https%3A%2F%2Fnilaus.atlassian.net%2Fwiki%2Fspaces%2FPM%2Fpages%2F1108803590%2FFactorio%2BS31%2B-%2BPost-Apocalypse&v=rDvowtkQuBY) Mods: Afraid of the dark - To make it possible to see what is going on at night Vehicle Snap - To be able to drive in a straight line Nilaus Crash Site Generator Tweak - custom made by TCNJ for this map Website: [http://nilaus.tv](https://www.youtube.com/redirect?event=video_description&redir_token=QUM4Zm9rVE5NYzQ1UGJOU1Vpa1dBbmsyaUxjLXxBR3JiS2Fsdjl0RFBxcktfRW1xM1I4TGh2cVd1NWdJeG

Factorio 0.17 Space Exploration #79 ROCKET LAUNCH FACILITY

49 Likes

3,818 Views

2019 Jun 26

SPACE EXPLORATION This series is focused on the new and exciting mod: Space Exploration by Earendel combined with the live integration with Twitch events through the TVC API by Timbo007up. 
*   Space Exploration mod allows you to leave the planet and build in orbit and settle new planets 
*   Movement is limited to the sacred path i.e. only on concrete (donations give concrete) 
*   Heavy focus on logistics through vehicles i.e. no miners or requester chests (vehicles limited by supporters) 
*   AAI Vehicles allow full automation of vehicles for transport 

 Streaming schedule: Monday or Tuesday at [20:00](http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V1yzFLIkIdc&t=1200s) CEST and Thursday at [20:00](http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V1yzFLIkIdc&t=1200s).
